1.3. Change Log
Version 2.8.1 a) Correction in Laser status return codes .
Version 2.8.0 b) ImportAI method has additional mandatory parameter for controlling the AI pre-processor stage.
Please note that all former SmartGraphCOM applicatchanged and recompiled to reflect the new methid syntax. ions that are using the ImportAI methid must be
Version 2.7.2 c) Template text changes in field command.
Version 2.7.0 d) Suppresses asynchronous xml messages sent by SmartGraph.
e) Get / Set command added to adjust template x/y position and rotation f) Get / set command added to retrieve and set template list for external template selection
g) Laser Status codes 130XX instead of 120XX
Version 2.6.1 h) Property m_bIsConnected added. This property indicates the connection status to SmartGraph.
Various method can return protNotConnected error code now.
Version 2.6.0 i) Method ImportTrace added to import pixel based graphics (bmp, jpg, gif, .) as vectorgraphics that
can be filled.. j) Update of font command (template language) to support usage of windows system fonts
Version 2.5.2 k) Methods added to relais protocol traffic from other SmartGraphCOM applications in order to share a
single SmartGraph application between multiple SmartGraphCOM applications. l) UTF string handling now independent of system Locale.
Version 2.3.0 m) Methods added to retrieve XML documents for database and Laser information
Version 2.2.0 n) Methods added to select, save and create databases
Version 2.1.0 o) Methods added to import and export parameter sets, configurations (product registrations) and
templates p) Method for version number retrieve (partially implemented)
Version 2.0.0 q) UTF-8 Unicode data format in methods SetTemplateText, AddTemplateText, SetVarPrompt,
SetVarList r) Methods added for inquiry of names of database objects ReadDatabaseObjects/GetObjectName and
variable names within templates s) Method added for retrieving template graphics in SVG format
Version 1.7.0 t) Methods added for MCL import

2. Introduction
SmartGraphCOM is an ActiveX component that comeenvironment. Beside the general programming interfacess as DLL to be integrated in any ActiveX capable to ActiveX enabled environments, SmartGraphCOM
features some general communications interfaces to subsystems. the PCs hardware, network or simple digital I/O
ACCor-2Fox
HTTP
Printer
CorDrelaw
HTTP
V.BNET
VC.+N+ET
Dephi
SmartGraphCOM needs the SmartGraph application running to carry out various operations on template databases offline and online on the Alltec Laser systems. It can control the Laser operation including start and
stop of Laser marking. With its capability to create new template graallowing to inspect and modify the template using SmartGraphs user interface, including setting of predefine phics from basic template language,
variables, SmartGraphCOM is an ideal component for Laser integration into flexible industrial environments.
The following document is a comprehensive users describes all object methods, their parameters and return codes. manual on the SmartGraphCOM interface methods. It
Along with the example code provided by Alltec, this allows for indepAlltec Lasers into customer specifendent development and integration of ic industrial control environments.
The following graphics illustrates the general architectuThis component interacts with corresponding layer in the SmartGraph application which in turn is connected to re for Alltec Laser integration using SmartGraphCOM.
the laser marking system. The following document will describe how to user the SmartGraphCOM interface and explain the return codes
from various communication layers

3.1. Properties
Property Description
m_bIsConnected Indicates a connection to SmartGraph executable
m_iLaserConnectTimeout Timeout for ConnectOnlineDB method to complete.
m_iCommandTimeout Timeout for all other methods to complete.
m_iNbObjects Number of objects in a given database (online or offline)
m_iOnOfflineDB Gets and sets current SmartGraph online / offline settings
m_strObjectNames Name of object of a given type (templat(online or offline) e, logo, font, ) in a given database

3.2. Methods
Method Description
SmartGraph connection methods
Connect Attaches SmartGraphCOM to a running SmartGraph application
Disconnect Detaches SmartGraphCOM from a running SmartGraph application
ProtocolHub Relais protocol traffic from other SmartGraphCOM applications to the actual connected SmartGraph
Database methods
ConnectOfflineDB Initiates SmartGraph to open a local PC file database.
ConnectOnlineDB Initiates SmartGraph to connect to a Laser system and opens its online database.
ReadDatabaseObjects Retrievs names of all objects stored in a database
ExportDBObject Exports a database object, such as template, parameter set or configuration to a file.
ImportDBObject Imports an object, such asor configuration from a file into the database. template, parameter set
CreateDatabase Creates a new offline database
SelectDatabase Selects either offline or online as the actual database
SaveDatabase Persitently saves content of a database
XML_GetDatabase Retrieves string containing the XML description of the selected database
GetTemplateList Gets the current template list for external template selection
SetTemplateList Sets the current template list for external template selection
Template methods
CreateTemplate Creates a new template within actual database.
DeleteTemplate Deletes a template from actual database.
SetActualTemplate Sets the actual template

Imports a logo from a Graphic Interchange Format file into the actual database
ImportTrace Imports a logo by vectorizing a pixel based input file such as bmp, jpg, gif, tif and png. Logos imported
with this method can be filled.
ImportMcl Imports a logo from a FOBA mcl file into the actual database
DeleteLogo Deletes a logo from the actual database
Object / Selection methods
GroupAllObjects Groups all objects in the current template.
GroupSelectedObjects Groups all selected objects (that have been inserted by the last AddTemplateText or SetTemplateText
call).
UngroupSelectedObjects Ungroups one or more selected objects.
Font methods
ImportTTF Imports a Font from an Windows installed True Type Font into the actual database
DeleteFont Deletes a font from the actual database
Variable methods
SetVarPrompt Sets a new value to a text variable (type Extern Text / Prompt) within the actual template of the current
database (online or offline)
SetVarDate Sets a new offset value to a date variable within the actual template of the current database (online or
offline)
SetVarTime Sets a new offset value to a time variable within the actual template of the current database (online or
offline)
SetVarSerial Sets a new value to a serial number variable within the actual template of the current database (online or
offline)
SetVarList Sets a new value to variables into a temporary list of variable setting without applying them.
SetVarListApply Applies the temporary list of variable values to a template and clears the list.
SetVarListClear Clears the temnporary list of variable settings without applying them to a template

4. AddTemplateText Method
Description Syntax Settings Return Values Remarks Specifics Example Applies To See Also
Description
The AddTemplateText inserts additional template language primitives into the specified template.
Syntax
object.AddTemplateText(strTemplateName , strTemplateText)
The AddTemplateText method syntax has these arguments:
Part Description
objectRequired. An object expression that evaluates to an object in the Applies To list.
strTemplateNameRequired. String expression specifying the template by name within the actual database.
strTemplateTextRequired. String expression specifying the template text (graphic primitive expressions) to be added to the template. Refer to Template Language documentation on further
details on template language elements.
Settings
(none)
Return Values
Constant Value Description
sgcOK 1 OK, operation successful.
sgcUnknownError -20000 Error code generated by SmartGraphCOM upon error conditions other than those listed below. Typically this code indicates
software or installation problems.
sgcTemplateNotFound -20007 This error indicates that the selected (named) template was not found upon execution of the method.
sgcConnectionError -20003 This error occurs with all methods that operate on an online database (connected to a LASER / marking system), if the
connection between SmartGraph and the marking system was lost or otherwise disrupted or the marking system does not
Compiled Verified Released Archive-Nb.:
On: 060731 on: on: Version Nb.: 2.8.1
C:\tmp\SmartGraphCOM_ProgrammersManual.doc
by: PK by: by:

FDS
SmartGraphCOM V2.8.1 Programmers Manual
SmartGraph Remote Control Component Page 21/362
sgcCommandError -20002 This error, generated by SmartGraph indicates an error in the received command, such as mismatch in number of parameters.
If versions of SmartGraph and SmartGraphCOM match, this error indicates software malfunction or reception of corrupted
data.
sgcTemplateTextError -20022 SmartGraph detects syntactical errors in template text.
Remarks
If AddTemplateText is invoked on the actual template, the following notes apply: All graphical objects that have been set to the template by SetTemplateText
, ImportTemplateText or AddTemplateText unselected prior to carry out the actual methods will be AddTemplateText method call. The newly added objects will be
selected entirely. This is important to understand withotherwise modify the current selection within the template editor. subsequent method calls that may center, rotate or
Specifics
(none)
Example
(none)
Applies To
SmartGraphCOM, SmartCOM
See Also
SetTemplateText Method
, CreateTemplate Method , ImportTemplateText Method

5. Connect Method
Description Syntax Settings Return Values Remarks Specifics Example Applies To See Also
Description
The Connect method attaches the SmartGraphCOM componentapplication. to a running instance of the SmartGraph
Syntax
object.Connect([strProtocol] [, strHostname] [,strPort])
The Connect method syntax has these arguments:
Part Description
objectRequired. An object expression that evaluates to an object in the Applies To list.
strProtocol Optional. String expression specifying the protocol to use. See settings for further information. If omitted, the default value for strProtocol is TCP
strHostnameOptional. String expression specifying the host to connect to. This host (PC) is the computer where SmartGraph is running. SmartGraph can run on the same PC as the
SmartControl container program or on any otherconnection. If omitted, the default value for PC that can be reached by tcp/ip LAN strHostname is 127.0.0.1
strPort Optional. Numeric expression specifying the tcp service port number. This is the port on which the SmartGraph application can accept tcp/ip connections. Default value for
strPort is 10888.
Settings
The strProtocolparameter settings are
Constant Value Description
(n.a.) TCP LAN connection will be used to connect to SmartGraph. The applied for this connection. This setting is default,TCP/IP protocol is
Return Values
Constant Value Description

SmartGraphCOM V2.8.1 Programmers Manual
SmartGraph Remote Control Component Page 23/362
sgcUnknownError -20000 Error code generated by SmartGraphCOM upon error conditions other than those listed below. Typically this code indicates
software or installation problems.
sckClosed-12000 Socket is closed.
sckConnectionPending -12003 Socket has pending connection.
sckResolvingHost -12004 Socket is resolving host name.
sckHostResolved -12005 Socket has resolved host name.
sckConnecting -12006 Socket is connecting.
sckClosing -12008 Socket is closing.
sckError -12009 Socket error occurred
sgcUnknownVersion -20012 The Connect methods negotiates the protocol version with the attached SmartGraph application. Any version mismatch
between SmartGraph and the related SmartGraphCOM object will result in this error message. Make sure you have updated
SmartGraph and the related SmartGraphCOM in sync.
Remarks
The Connect method attaches the SmartGraphCOM component, ora running instance of SmartGraph. This means the Smar to be precise, its container application to tGraph application must be started prior to any use
of the Connect method. The SmartGraph application may be executed on the same host (PC) as the container application or on any other PC that may be reached via tcp/ip.
Typical applications may run the SmartGraph applicatsame PC. Here, the programmer has three choices to address SmartGraph on the same host: ion and SmarGraphCOM conmtainer application on the
hostname = localhost (default) hostname= 127.0.0.1
hostname= aaa.bbb.ccc.ddd which is the IP address of the local PC
These three alternatives differ in connection speed: While a) might be the slowest alternative, b) might possibly the fastest alternative. Providing 127.0.0.1 is the preferred way of connecting to SmartGraph if
running on the same PC

10. CreateParameterIndex Method
Description Syntax Settings Return Values Remarks Specifics Example Applies To See Also
Description
The CreateParameterIndex inserts a new index level into the parameter set specified
Syntax
object.CreateParameterIndex(strParameterSetName, iSourceIndex )
The CreateParameterIndex method syntax has these arguments:
Part Description
objectRequired. An object expression that evaluates to an object in the Applies To list.
strParameterSetNameRequired. String expression specifying the name of the new parameter set.
iSourceIndexOptional. String expression . The new index level will be constructed by copying all parameter values from the source index level. Default is 1.
Settings
(none)
Return Values
Constant Value Description
sgcOK 1 OK, operation successful.
sgcUnknownError -20000 Error code generated by SmartGraphCOM upon error conditions other than those listed below. Typically this
code indicates software or installation problems.
sgcCommandError -20002 This error, generated by SmartGraph indicates an error in the received command, such as mismatch in number
of parameters. If versions of SmartGraph and SmartGraphCOM match, this error indicates software
malfunction or reception of corrupted data.
Compiled Verified Released Archive-Nb.:
On: 060731 on: on: Version Nb.: 2.8.1
C:\tmp\SmartGraphCOM_ProgrammersManual.doc
by: PK by: by:

FDS
SmartGraphCOM V2.8.1 Programmers Manual
SmartGraph Remote Control Component Page 37/362
sgcParameterSetNotFound -20005 The specified parameter set was not found in the actual database. Refer to SmartGraph documentation
on defining and managing parameter sets.
sgcTemplateInUseError -20028 Attempt to change a parameter set that is currently being used in an active template being marked on the
laser.
sgcParemeterIndexNotFoundError -20031 Attempt to refer to a parameter index level that was not defined within parameter set.
Remarks
A parameter set cannot be changed while marking of template is going on that references the parameter set.
Currently there is a system imposed maximum of 10 index levels per parameter set.
Specifics
(none)
Example
The following Visual Basic code connects to SmartGraph and creates a new parameter set (MyParameter2) based on the standard parameter set Paper and a total of 9 new index levels based upon the default index
level 1.
Public Sub CreateParameterIndices() Dim lRet As Long
 Dim I As Integer Dim sg As CSmartGraph
 Set sg = New CSmartGraph 'create SmartGraphCOM instance lRet = sg.Connect() 'connect to SmartGraph
 lRet = sg.ConnectOfflineDB("c:\Standard.ydb") 'connect to selected offline database lRet = sg.CreateParameterSet("MyParameter2", "Paper")
 For I = 2 To 10 'currently there is a maximum of 10 index levels per parameter set lRet = sg.CreateParameterIndex("MyParameter2", 1)
 Next I lRet = sg.Disconnect() 'disconnect from SmartGraph
 Set sg = Nothing 'clean up ActiveX Component End Sub
As a result, the parameter dialog window shows a total of 10 index levels for the new parameter set. All parameter values are the same as those specified for index level 1.

Specifics
(none)
Example
The following Visual Basic code connects to SmartGraph (MyParameter2). This assumes the existence of the and deletes a total of 5 indices from parameter set parameter set and at least 6 indices defined within the
parameter set. Refer to example code of CreateParameterIndex Method
 on how to create index levels.
Public Sub DeleteParameterIndices() Dim lRet As Long
 Dim I As Integer Dim sg As CSmartGraph
 'make sure parameter set is defined and has more than 5 index levels 'run CreateParameterIndices first for instance
 Set sg = New CSmartGraph 'create SmartGraphCOM instance lRet = sg.Connect() 'connect to SmartGraph
 lRet = sg.ConnectOfflineDB("c:\Standard.ydb") 'connect to selected offline database For I = 1 To 5 'Assuming there are more than 5 indices defined
 lRet = sg.DeleteParameterIndex("MyParameter2") Next I
 lRet = sg.Disconnect() 'disconnect from SmartGraph Set sg = Nothing 'clean up ActiveX Component
End Sub
As a result, the parameter dialog window shows the remaining index levels for the parameter set (MyParameter2).

sgcStartDist 4 Specifies the distance of the first product from being readily placed for marking at the point in time when start trigger
occurs.
sgcLockDist 5 Specifies the minimum distance between two products.

The following Visual Basic code connects to SmartGraph, and retrieves the individual property settings from configuration MyConfiguration2 (refer to Example
 of SetConfigurationValue Method a parameters. The example code displays the result in an internal list box. on how to create and set
Public Sub GetConfigurationValues() Dim lRet As Long
 Dim sg As CSmartGraph
 Set sg = New CSmartGraph 'create SmartGraphCOM instance lRet = sg.Connect() 'connect to SmartGraph
 lRet = sg.ConnectOfflineDB("c:\Standard.ydb") 'connect to offline database With SmartGraphCOM_SamplesForm.ConfigurationListBox
 .Clear .AddItem "StartDelay: " & vbTab & sg.GetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcStartDelay)
 .AddItem "LockDelay: " & vbTab & sg.GetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcLockDelay) .AddItem "StopDelay: " & vbTab & sg.GetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcStopDelay)
 .AddItem "StartDist: " & vbTab & sg.GetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcStartDist) .AddItem "LockDist: " & vbTab & sg.GetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcLockDist)
 .AddItem "StopDist: " & vbTab & sg.GetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcStopDist) .AddItem "ProductMovement: " & vbTab & _
 sg.GetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcProductMovement) .AddItem "ProductAngle: " & vbTab & _
 sg.GetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcProductAngle) .AddItem "ProductSpeed: " & vbTab & _
 sg.GetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcProductSpeed) .AddItem "PulsesPerRotation: " & vbTab & _
 sg.GetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcEncoderPulsesPerRotation) .AddItem "Dist.PerRotation: " & vbTab &_
 sg.GetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcEncoderDistancePerRotation) .AddItem "RotationDirection: " & vbTab & _
 sg.GetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcEncoderRotationDirection) .AddItem "StartTrigger: " & vbTab & _
 sg.GetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcStartTrigger) .AddItem "ConsTrigger: " & vbTab & _
 sg.GetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcConsTrigger) .AddItem "StopTrigger: " & vbTab & _
 sg.GetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcStopTrigger) End With
 lRet = sg.Disconnect() 'disconnect from SmartGraph Set sg = Nothing 'clean up ActiveX Component
End Sub

Description Syntax Settings Return Values Remarks Specifics Example Applies To See Also
Description
The GetGeometry method rotates the collectivity of all selecttemplate to the angle specified with the ed objects that have been placed on the active GetGeometry method.
Syntax
object. GetGeometry ( dblX, dblY, dblWidth, dlbHeight, dblAngle )
The GetGeometry method syntax has these arguments:
Part Description
objectRequired. An object expression that evaluates to an object in the Applies To list.
dblXRequired. Reference to variable of type double. Returns the X position of lower left corner of bounding box.
dblYRequired. Reference to variable of type double. Returns the Y position of lower left corner of bounding box.
dblWidthRequired. Reference to variable of type double. Returns the width of the bounding box.
dblHeightRequired. Reference to variable of type double. Returns the height of the bounding box.
dblAngleRequired. Reference to variable of type double. Returns the rotation angle.

(none)

software or installation problems.

Public Sub GetGeometry() Dim lRet As Long
 Dim sg As CSmartGraph Dim dblX As Double
 Dim dblY As Double Dim dblWidth As Double
 Dim dblHeight As Double Dim dblAngle As Double

 lRet = sg.SetTemplateText("MyTemplate7", "Arc(5,5,0,360,ARC_CLOSE, 5,5)" & _ "Rect(10,10:10:10:0)" & _
 "Abs(-10,-10) ""A"" ")
 lRet = sg.GroupSelectedObjects() lRet = sg.SetRotation(30)
 lRet = sg.GetGeometry(dblX, dblY, dblWidth, dblHeight, dblAngle) With SmartGraphCOM_SamplesForm.GeometryListBox
 .Clear .AddItem "X-Pos: " & vbTab & dblX & " mm"
 .AddItem "Y-Pos: " & vbTab & dblY & " mm" .AddItem "Width: " & vbTab & dblWidth & " mm"
 .AddItem "Height: " & vbTab & dblHeight & " mm" .AddItem "Rot.: " & vbTab & dblAngle & " deg"
 End With lRet = sg.Disconnect() 'disconnect from SmartGraph
 Set sg = Nothing 'clean up ActiveX Component

Description Syntax Settings Return Values Remarks Specifics Example Applies To See Also

Public Sub GroupSelection() Dim lRet As Long

 lRet = sg.AddTemplateText("MyTemplate", "Arc(5,5,0,360,ARC_CLOSE, -20,-10)") lRet = sg.AddTemplateText("MyTemplate", "Arc(5,5,0,360,ARC_CLOSE, -20,0)")
 lRet = sg.AddTemplateText("MyTemplate", "Arc(5,5,0,360,ARC_CLOSE, -10,-10)") lRet = sg.AddTemplateText("MyTemplate", "Arc(5,5,0,360,ARC_CLOSE, -10,0)")

 lRet = sg.GroupAllObjects() lRet = sg.Disconnect() 'disconnect from SmartGraph
 Set sg = Nothing 'clean up ActiveX Component End Sub

35. ImportAi Method
Description Syntax Settings Return Values Remarks Specifics Example Applies To See Also
Description
The ImportAi method imports an Adobe Illustrator graphics fpreprocessing the AI input data. ile. Import can be carried out with or without
Syntax
object.ImportAi(strLogoName ,
strParameters , strFileName])
The ImportAi method syntax has these arguments:
Part Description
objectRequired. An object expression that evaluates to an object in the Applies To list.
strLogoNameRequired. String expression specifying the logo name within the actual database.
strParameters Required. Specifies a set of parameters to be used by the AI import engine. See settings for details.
strFileNameRequired. String expression specifying the absolute path of the Adobe Illustrator (*.ai) file for import into actual database.
Settings
The strParameters settings are
Format Value Description
Size based operation -F PLOT-AI THRESHOLD t If strParameters is set to the constant string -F PLOT-AI THRESHOLD  followed by an interger value 0100..
This option decomposes all AI structures including text into simple objects. These objects are rendered onto a virtual
canvas. The edges of all areas having a gray value smaller than THRESOLD are considered fillable areas. These
areas are imported into SmartGraph. All areas with a gray value greater than THRESOLD parameter are ignored. One
of the most obvious effects of this option is preserving of text including font shapes without the need to import the
font into SmartGraph. Furthermore all lines in the AI file appear in SmartGrah with the same thickness  lines are
decomposed into fillable areas as well  preserving the
Compiled Verified Released Archive-Nb.:
On: 060731 on: on: Version Nb.: 2.8.1
C:\tmp\SmartGraphCOM_ProgrammersManual.doc
by: PK by: by:

FDS
SmartGraphCOM V2.8.1 Programmers Manual
SmartGraph Remote Control Component Page 112/362
No prprocessing-F PLAIN-AI If strParameters is set to the constant string -F PLAIN-AI no preprocessing of the AI file takes place. This is the
preferred mode for fast one-to-one original AI import. If the results differ from expected results  especially if
included text does not appear as desired, line widths matters or overlayed objects are causing problem: use F
PLOT-Ai THRESHOLD t parameter (see above) instead.
Return Values
Constant Value Description
sgcOK 1 OK, operation successful.
sgcUnknownError -20000 Error code generated by SmartGraphCOM upon error conditions other than those listed below. Typically this code indicates
software or installation problems.
sgcConnectionError -20003 This error occurs with all methods that operate on an online database (connected to a LASER / marking system), if the
connection between SmartGraph and the marking system was lost or otherwise disrupted or the marking system does not
adequately respond to commands issued by SmartGraph.
sgcFileOpenError -20017 File specified in one of the import methods does not exit or cant be opened or causes error upon reading and importing.
sgcCommandError -20002 This error, generated by SmartGraph indicates an error in the received command, such as mismatch in number of parameters.
If versions of SmartGraph and SmartGraphCOM match, this error indicates software malfunction or reception of corrupted
data.
Remarks
The imported logo can be used (referenced) by any Currently number of templates from within the database. ImportAi does not support PS1 or PS2 font usage in the ai file. All text must be converted to vectors
within the ai file. This can be achieved by setting the appropriate save/export setting in Adobe Illustrator or other third party graphic applications.
Any import of graphic files, logos, fonts or other datSetActualTemplatea files will reset the actual template. This means
 should be called after all required imports have been carried out.
Specifics
Compiled Verified Released Archive-Nb.: thickness of the original lines. Please not that this option reuires substantial computing resources and should be
used if F PALIN-AI (see below) does not provode desired results.

C:\tmp\SmartGraphCOM_ProgrammersManual.doc

The following SmartGraphCOM VB code imports this crlogo names Cross-Plain and Cross-decomposed. The fioss.ai graphics into SmartGraph two times using the rst call simply imports the graphical AI primitives
without decomposing while the latter decomposes and re-assembles the graphics into filleed areas.
Public Sub AiPlainPlotTest() Dim i As Integer
 Dim j As Integer Dim lRet As Long
 Set sg = New CSmartGraph lRet = sg.Connect
 sg.m_iCommandTimeout = 20 lRet = sg.ImportAi("Cross-Plain", "-F PLAIN-AI", "c:\alltec-pk\GraphicFiles\AI\cross.ai")
 lRet = sg.ImportAi("Cross-Decomposed", "-F PLOT-AI -THRESHOLD 50", "c:\alltec-pk\GraphicFiles\AI\cross.ai")
 lRet = sg.Disconnect Set sg = Nothing
End Sub
The resulting imported logos in SmartGraph look as follows:

The following VB program imports the AI logo in plain mode (no preprocessing) and than 5 times using different threshold parameters from 90 to 10 in steps of 20.
Public Sub AiThresholdTest() Dim i As Integer
 Dim j As Integer Dim lRet As Long
 Set sg = New CSmartGraph lRet = sg.Connect
 sg.m_iCommandTimeout = 60 lRet = sg.ImportAi("Gray-Plain", "-F PLAIN-AI", "c:\gray.ai")
 lRet = sg.ImportAi("Gray-Thres-90", "-F PLOT-AI -THRESHOLD 90", "c:\gray.ai") lRet = sg.ImportAi("Gray-Thres-70", "-F PLOT-AI -THRESHOLD 70", "c:\gray.ai")
 lRet = sg.ImportAi("Gray-Thres-50", "-F PLOT-AI -THRESHOLD 50", "c:\gray.ai") lRet = sg.ImportAi("Gray-Thres-30", "-F PLOT-AI -THRESHOLD 30", "c:\gray.ai")
 lRet = sg.ImportAi("Gray-Thres-10", "-F PLOT-AI -THRESHOLD 10", "c:\gray.ai") lRet = sg.Disconnect
 Set sg = Nothing End Sub

36. ImportBmp Method
Description Syntax Settings Return Values Remarks Specifics Example Applies To See Also
Description
The ImportBmp method imports a BMP (Windows Bitmap) graphics file.
Syntax
object.ImportBmp(strLogoName, iMode, iValue, strFileName])
The ImportBmp method syntax has these arguments:
Part Description
objectRequired. An object expression that evaluates to an object in the Applies To list.
strLogoNameRequired. String expression specifying the logo name within the actual database.
iMode Required. Specifies the colour/grey scale depth reduction algorithm. See Settings for further information.
iValue Required. Specifies the input parameter for colour/grey scale depth reduction algorithm
strFileNameRequired. String expression specifying the absolute path of the Windows bitmap file for import into actual database.
Settings
The iMode / iValue settings are
Constant Value Description
sgcThreshold 1 Colours will be mapped to their corresponding luminance value 0255 in the first step. The second step maps all luminance values below value specified in
iValue into black and all value above iValue into white pixels.
sgcDither 2 (Currently not available)
sgcOptimize 3 (Currently not available)
Return Values
Compiled Verified Released Archive-Nb.:
On: 060731 on: on: Version Nb.: 2.8.1
C:\tmp\SmartGraphCOM_ProgrammersManual.doc
by: PK by: by:

FDS
SmartGraphCOM V2.8.1 Programmers Manual
SmartGraph Remote Control Component Page 117/362
sgcOK 1 OK, operation successful.
sgcUnknownError -20000 Error code generated by SmartGraphCOM upon error conditions other than those listed below. Typically this code indicates
software or installation problems.
sgcConnectionError -20003 This error occurs with all methods that operate on an online database (connected to a LASER / marking system), if the
connection between SmartGraph and the marking system was lost or otherwise disrupted or the marking system does not
adequately respond to commands issued by SmartGraph.
sgcFileOpenError -20017 File specified in one of the import methods does not exit or cant be opened or causes error upon reading and importing.
sgcCommandError -20002 This error, generated by SmartGraph indicates an error in the received command, such as mismatch in number of parameters.
If versions of SmartGraph and SmartGraphCOM match, this error indicates software malfunction or reception of corrupted
data.
Remarks
The imported logo can be used (referenced) by any number of templates from within the database.
Any import of graphic files, logos, fonts or other datSetActualTemplatea files will reset the actual template. This means
 should be called after all required imports have been carried out.

Public Sub ImportDBObjectTest() Dim lRet As Long
 Dim sg As CSmartGraph Dim strTemplateName As String
 Set sg = New CSmartGraph lRet = sg.Connect("")
lRet = sg.ImportDBObject(sgcTemplate, "Alltec_NEW", _ "c:\dbobjects\MyTemplate.tpl", True)
lRet = sg.ImportDBObject(sgcParameterSet, "Paper_NEW",_ "c:\dbobjects\MyParameter.par", True)
lRet = sg.ImportDBObject(sgcConfiguration, "Allprint_NEW", _ "c:\dbobjects\MyConfiguration.prd", True)
 lRet = sg.Disconnect() 'disconnect from SmartGraph Set sg = Nothing 'clean up ActiveX Component
End Sub

41. ImportTrace Method
Description Syntax Settings Return Values Remarks Specifics Example Applies To See Also
Description
The ImportTrace method imports pixel based image files (BMP, logo. The vectorized logo consists of closed polygons JPG, TIF, GIF, PNG) as vectorized graphics that can be filled using the standard filling methods.
Syntax
object.ImportTrace(strLogoName, strParameters, strFileName])
The ImportTrace method syntax has these arguments:
Part Description
ObjectRequired. An object expression that evaluates to an object in the Applies To list.
strLogoNameRequired. String expression specifying the logo name within the actual database.
strParameters Required. Specifies a set of parameters to be used by the trace import engine. See settings
 for details.
strFileNameRequired. String expression specifying the absolute path of the image file for import into actual database. The file must have one of the extensions .BMP, .JPG, .GIF, .TIF
or .PNG for one of the supported formatuncompressed or RLE-compressed), JPEG / s: Windos bitmap files (files can be Joint Photographic Expert Group, Graphics
Interchange Format, Tag Image Format or Protable Network Graphic
Settings
The strParameters settings are
Format Value Description
Size based operation -F PIXELTRACE SIZE w h strParameters is a string consisting of a constand header -F PIXELTRACE  followed by a box size specification -
SIZE w h where w specifies the width in mm and height in mm. The import engine scales the resulting logo h the
to fit into the specified box (waspect ratio of the image. This is the preferred method if x h) without changing the
different images of unknown size and format shall be placed in a predefined location.

Return Values
Constant Value Description
sgcOK 1 OK, operation successful.
sgcUnknownError -20000 Error code generated by SmartGraphCOM upon error conditions other than those listed below. Typically this code indicates
software or installation problems.
sgcConnectionError -20003 This error occurs with all methods that operate on an online database (connected to a LASER / marking system), if the
connection between SmartGraph and the marking system was lost or otherwise disrupted or the marking system does not
adequately respond to commands issued by SmartGraph.
sgcFileOpenError -20017 File specified in one of the import methods does not exit or cant be opened or causes error upon reading and importing.
sgcCommandError -20002 This error, generated by SmartGraph indicates an error in the received command, such as mismatch in number of parameters.
If versions of SmartGraph and SmartGraphCOM match, this error indicates software malfunction or reception of corrupted
data.
Remarks
The ImportTrace method is intended for simple graphic files that consist of reasonable amount of files such a symbols, company logos and other image structural elements such as lines and areas.
The method is NOT intended for complex graphics such as photo images (landscapes, portraits of persons, etc). These image contents typically result in a huge amount of small areas that must be filled with tiny vector
strokes in SmartGraph. Although the algorithms can process these image contents, the SmartGraph processing time to render in the logos would be unacceptable high.
Any color information in the imported files will be revertthreshold (0.45) is applied to map gray scale ed to gray scale in a first step. In a second step, a into black/white suitable for laser etching.
Any import of graphic files, logos, fonts or other datSetActualTemplatea files will reset the actual template. This means
 should be called after all required imports have been carried out.
Compiled Verified Released Resolution based Archive-Nb.: -F PIXELTRACE DPI r strParameters is a string consisting of a constand header -F PIXELTRACE  followed by a resolution specification -
operation DPI r where r specifies the resolution of the pixel image in dots (pixels) per inch. Both horizontal and vertical
resolution is assumed to given by the imported logo can be calculated by the number of r. The resulting size of
horizontal and vertical pixels along with the This is the preferred method if different images of unknown r parameter.
size shall be imported preserving the size of the image.

Public Sub PixelTraceTest() Dim lRet As Long
 Dim sg As CSmartGraph
 Set sg = New CSmartGraph lRet = sg.Connect()
 sg.m_iCommandTimeout = 20
 lRet = sg.ImportTrace("Alltec_30x150", "-F PIXELTRACE -SIZE 30.000 150.00", "C:\Alltec_BW.jpg") lRet = sg.ImportTrace("Alltec_150x30", "-F PIXELTRACE -SIZE 150.000 30.00", "C:\Alltec_BW.jpg")
 lRet = sg.ImportTrace("Alltec_600dpi", "-F PIXELTRACE -DPI 600", "C:\Alltec_BW.jpg") lRet = sg.SetTemplateText("MyTemplate",
 "Rect(-100,-75:30:150:0) Abs(-100,-5) Logo(""Alltec_30x150"") ") lRet = sg.AddTemplateText("MyTemplate",
 "Rect(-70,0:150:30:0) Abs(-40,0) Logo(""Alltec_150x30"")") lRet = sg.AddTemplateText("MyTemplate",
 "Rect(0,-80:88:28:0) Abs(0,-80) Logo(""Alltec_600dpi"")") lRet = sg.Disconnect
 Set sg = Nothing End Sub
As a result, SmartGraph displays the objects as follows:

TTF fonts.
strStyle Optional. String expression . Specifies the font style such as bold. Note: If strStyle parameter is given, the strScript parameter must also be supplied.
strScript Optional. String expression . Specifies the font script such as western. Note: If strScript parameter is given, the strStyle parameter must also be supplied.

Syntax

Part Description

iSecondsPositive integer numeric expression. Specifies the maximum number of seconds for an attempt to connect to as Laser marking system. Default is 60 seconds.
Settings
(none)
Return Values
Returns the actual timeout for establishing Laser connections in seconds.
Remarks
When SmartGraph connects to a Laser system the onparsed by SmartGraph. This operation might involve someline database stored in the Laser will be read and time because the Laser system might be busy with
a marking job. During this high system load times the Laser marking system provides the database to SmartGraph with a low priority, hence the operation might span for a longer time than other operations.
Because of this, the timeout for this specific operation can be adjusted separately from all other operation methods timeouts.

50. ProtocolHub
Description Syntax Settings Return Values Remarks Specifics Example Applies To See Also
Description
The ProtocolHub method sends a preformtted commandstring (output from another instance of SmartGraphCOM to the SmartGraph application and retrieves the response string from SmartGraph.
This method allows for daisy chaining SmartGraphSmartGraph. This methods tunnels the protocol trCOM applicationsthat share a single instance of affic between (another) SmartGraphCOM application and
the SmartGraph server, prviding relais capabilities to the actual SmartGraphCOM application.
Syntax
object.ProtocolHub( strCommand, strResponse)
The ProtocolHub method syntax has these arguments:
Part Description
objectRequired. An object expression that evaluates to an object in the Applies To list.
strCommandRequired. String expression . The command string as received from another SmartGraphCOM application. This string will be sent to the SmartGraph
strResponse Required. String variable. The response string (including status or error codes, data and other) as generated by SmartGraph as a resut / response to the command string.
Settings
(none)
Return Values
Constant Value Description
protOK 1 OK, operation successful.
protUnknownError -15000 Any error condition in protocol engine that is not covered by subsequent error code. This typically indicates software and/or
data transport issues.
protInvalidResponse -15001 Protocol engine received a message block from SmartGraph which is syntactically OK, but which specifies a command that is
no valid response to the previous command issued by
Compiled Verified Released Archive-Nb.:
On: 060731 on: on: Version Nb.: 2.8.1
C:\tmp\SmartGraphCOM_ProgrammersManual.doc
by: PK by: by:

FDS
SmartGraphCOM V2.8.1 Programmers Manual
SmartGraph Remote Control Component Page 154/362
protNoResponse -15002 Timeout condition: Protocol engine did not receive any response from SmartGraph within the timeout period.
protNoResponseCommand -15003 Protocol engine received a message block from SmartGraph having no command delimiter character(s).
protResponseTooShort -15004 Protocol engine received a message block from SmartGraph that specifies a total length that cannot fit any useful command.
protInvalidNbArguments -15005 Protocol engine received a message block from SmartGraph having a wrong number or arguments.
protNoCommand -15006 Protocol engine received a message block from SmartGraph having a command with or more white space characters only.
protZeroLenCommand -15007 Protocol engine received a message block from SmartGraph with a command of length Zero
protNonNumericErrorCode -15008 Protocol engine received error message from SmartGraph counterpart, but error code is non-numeric.
protMissingCharacters -15009 Frame of message block indicates more characters nneded than those received.
protTrailingCharacters -15010 Protocol engine detected trailing characters behind correct framed message block

Description

Public Sub SelectDBTest(iOnOffline As Integer) Dim lRet As Long
 Set sg = New CSmartGraph 'create SmartGraphCOM instance lRet = sg.Connect() 'connect to SmartGraph
 lRet = sg.ConnectOnlineDB() 'connect to Laser online database lRet = sg.ConnectOfflineDB() 'connect to PC offline database
 lRet = sg.SelectDatabase(sgcOnline) lRet = sg.Disconnect() 'disconnect from SmartGraph
 Set sg = Nothing 'clean up ActiveX Component End Sub

Description Syntax Settings Return Values Remarks Specifics Example Applies To See Also
Description
The SetConfigurationValue sets a new value to the specified coconfiguration). nfiguration parameter (within the named
Syntax

The SetConfigurationValue method syntax has these arguments:
Part Description

strConfigurationNameRequired. String expression specifies the name of the configuration.

Settings

Constant Value Description
sgcStartDelay 1 Specifies the time span between the start trigger event and the first product to be readily placed for marking.
sgcLockDelay2 Specifies the minimum time span between two products to become available for marking.
sgcStopDelay3 Minimum time span between last product and occurrence of end trigger condition.
sgcStartDist 4 Specifies the distance of the first product from being readily placed for marking at the point in time when start trigger

Example
The following Visual Basic code connects to SmartGraph, creates a new configuration named MyConfiguration2 and sets the individual configuration parameters to some values, that can be visually
recognized very easily. These setting are not necessarily useful in real life applications.
Public Sub SetConfigurationValues() Dim lRet As Long
 Dim sg As CSmartGraph
 Set sg = New CSmartGraph 'create SmartGraphCOM instance lRet = sg.Connect() 'connect to SmartGraph
 lRet = sg.ConnectOfflineDB("c:\Standard.ydb") 'connect to selected offline database lRet = sg.CreateConfiguration("MyConfiguration2")
 lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcStartDelay, 1111) lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcLockDelay, 2222)
 lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcStopDelay, 3333) lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcStartDist, 4444444)
 lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcLockDist, 5555555) lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcStopDist, 6666666)
 lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcProductMovement, sgcProductMoveEncoder) lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcProductAngle, 222222)
 lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcProductSpeed, 7777777) lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcEncoderPulsesPerRotation, 8888)
 lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcEncoderDistancePerRotation, 999999) lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcEncoderRotationDirection, sgcEncoderLeft)
 lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcStartTrigger, sgcTriggerMode1Raise) lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcConsTrigger, sgcTriggerMode2Fall)
 lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcStopTrigger, sgcTriggerMode3Raise) lRet = sg.Disconnect() 'disconnect from SmartGraph
 Set sg = Nothing 'clean up ActiveX Component End Sub
As a result, the configuration dialog window shows the new settings for MyConfiguration2.

SmartGraph documentation on variable types such as serial numbers, dates, text, etc.

Date variables showing default values
The following VB code sets these variables to an offset of 3 days, 3 weeks, 3 months and 3 years
Public Sub SetVarDateTest() Dim sg As CSmartGraph
 Dim lRet As Long
 Set sg = New CSmartGraph 'Create COM Component lRet = sg.Connect() 'onnect to SmartGraph
 lRet = sg.ConnectOfflineDB("c:\a.ydb") 'predefined databse lRet = sg.SetActualTemplate("DateTest") 'predefined template
 lRet = sg.SetVarDate("MyDate1", 3, sgcDaysUnit) 'set new offset (3 days) to variable Mydate1 lRet = sg.SetVarDate("MyDate2", 3, sgcWeeksUnit) 'set new offset (3 weeks) to variable Mydate2
 lRet = sg.SetVarDate("MyDate3", 3, sgcMonthsUnit) 'set new offset (3 months) to variable Mydate3 lRet = sg.SetVarDate("MyDate4", 3, sgcYearsUnit) 'set new offset (3 years) to variable Mydate4
 lRet = sg.Disconnect() 'disconnect from SmartGraph Set sg = Nothing 'clean up COM Component
End Sub
As a result the new variable settings can bee seen in SmartGraphs marking dialog:

69. SetVarList Method
Description Syntax Settings Return Values Remarks Specifics Example Applies To See Also
Description
The SetVarList sets multiple new values to variables in any given online or offline template.
Syntax
object.SetVarList( iType, strVarName, strValue [, iUnit] )
The SetVarList method syntax has these arguments:
Part Description
objectRequired. An object expression that evaluates to an object in the Applies To list.
iTypeRequired. Integer expression . Specifies the type of the variable to be set. See Settings below for further details. This type was set upon definition of the variable in the template.
strVarName Required. String expression . Specifies the name of the variable to be set. This variable must be defined in the named template. Any integer values required for date / time offset
variables must be specified as string expression.
strValue Required. String expression . Specifies the new content setting of the named variablet.
iUnit Optional. Integer expression . Specifies the unit of the given value. See Settings for further details below
Settings
The iType settings are
Constant Value Description
sgcSerialType 2 The variable is of type Serial Number(see below), if the variable is of type Serial. There is no strUnit parameter
sgcPromptType3 The variable is of type Extern Text(see below), if the variable is of type Prompt . There is no strUnit parameter
sgcDateType4 The variable is of type Date Offset. See possible below. strUnit parameters
sgcTimeType5 The variable is of type Time Offsetparameters below. . See possible strUnit

Specifics
The SetVarList method set the variables along with the types, values and units into a temporary (in-process) storage place. Any error that may occur while seting those variables will not effect the variables set prior to this
error. They will remain available in the internal SmartGraphCOM storage. The list of variable settings will be cleared automatically after call of SetVarListApply
method. or SetVarListClear

 lRet = sg.SetVarList(sgcPromptType, "MyPrompt1", "abcdefg") lRet = sg.SetVarList(sgcSerialType, "MySerial1", "333")
 lRet = sg.SetVarList(sgcTimeType, "MyTime1", "33", sgcHoursUnit) lRet = sg.SetVarList(sgcDateType, "MyDate1", "22", sgcDaysUnit)

Prompt variables showing their default values
The VB code first sets these three variables to new values: abc, def and ghi without applying them to the template. Than the method SetVarListClear is called and one of the variables (MyPrompt2) is set to the string
123. These settings are applied to the template.
Public Sub SetVarListClearTest() Dim sg As CSmartGraph
 Dim lRet As Long
 Set sg = New CSmartGraph lRet = sg.Connect()
 lRet = sg.ConnectOfflineDB("c:\d.ydb") lRet = sg.SetActualTemplate("SetVarListClearTest")
 lRet = sg.SetVarList(sgcPromptType, "MyPrompt1", "abc") lRet = sg.SetVarList(sgcPromptType, "MyPrompt2", "def")
 lRet = sg.SetVarList(sgcPromptType, "MyPrompt3", "ghi") lRet = sg.SetVarListClear
 lRet = sg.SetVarList(sgcPromptType, "MyPrompt2", "123") lRet = sg.SetVarListApply(sgcOffLine, "SetVarListClearTest")
 lRet = sg.Disconnect() 'disconnect from SmartGraph Set sg = Nothing 'clean up ActiveX Component
End Sub
As a result the new variable settings can bee seen in SmartGraphs marking dialog:

with a single method call, while the last four circles are placed AddTemplateText
 call. The program groups all objects of the temlplate and than un-groups them again.
Public Sub GroupSelection() Dim lRet As Long
Dim sg As CSmartGraph
 Set sg = New CSmartGraph 'create SmartGraphCOM instance lRet = sg.Connect() 'connect to SmartGraph
 lRet = sg.ConnectOfflineDB("C:\Standard.ydb") 'connect to an offline database lRet = sg.CreateTemplate("MyTemplate", "Paper", "Allprint", True)
 lRet = sg.AddTemplateText("MyTemplate", "Arc(5,5,0,360,ARC_CLOSE, -20,-10)") lRet = sg.AddTemplateText("MyTemplate", "Arc(5,5,0,360,ARC_CLOSE, -20,0)")
 lRet = sg.AddTemplateText("MyTemplate", "Arc(5,5,0,360,ARC_CLOSE, -10,-10)") lRet = sg.AddTemplateText("MyTemplate", "Arc(5,5,0,360,ARC_CLOSE, -10,0)")
 lRet = sg.AddTemplateText("MyTemplate", "Arc(5,5,0,360,ARC_CLOSE, 0,-10) " & _ "Arc(5,5,0,360,ARC_CLOSE, 0,0) " & _
 "Arc(5,5,0,360,ARC_CLOSE, 10,-10) " & _ "Arc(5,5,0,360,ARC_CLOSE, 10,0)")
 lRet = sg.GroupAllObjects() lRet = sg.UngroupSelectedObjects()
 lRet = sg.Disconnect() 'disconnect from SmartGraph Set sg = Nothing 'clean up ActiveX Component
End Sub
The result of the sample code are eight individual circle objects. None of them are grouped into compound (grouped) objects. All object are still selected after ungrouping in SmartGraph editor:

These are the return codes generated by the normal apsituations of possible codes, some plication layer, In general these codes describe of them during normal execution, some of them indication unexpected error
conditions in the software or the data transport mechanism.
Constant Value Description
sgcOK 1 OK, operation successful.
sgcUnknownError -20000 Error code generated by SmartGraphCOM upon error conditions other than those listed below. Typically this
code indicates software or installation problems.
sgcUndefinedError -20001 Error code generated by SmartGraph application upon error conditions other than those listed below.
Typically this code indicates software or installation problems.
sgcCommandError -20002 This error, generated by SmartGraph indicates an error in the received command, such as mismatch in
number of parameters. If versions of SmartGraph and SmartGraphCOM match, this error indicates software
malfunction or reception of corrupted data.
sgcConnectionError -20003 This error occurs with all methods that operate on an online database (connected to a LASER / marking
system), if the connection between SmartGraph and the marking system was lost or otherwise disrupted or
the marking system does not adequately respond to commands issued by SmartGraph.
sgcTemplateAlreadyExists -20004 Upon creation of a new template, SmartGraph detects that a template already exists with the name specified.
Under this circumstances the error is only generated, if the flag bOverwrite was set to False
sgcParameterSetNotFound -20005 The specified parameter set was not found in the actual database. Refer to SmartGraph documentation
on defining and managing parameter sets.
sgcConfigurationNotFound -20006 The specified configuration was not found in the actual database. Refer to SmartGraph documentation on
defining and managing configurations.
sgcTemplateNotFound -20007 This error indicates that the selected (named) template was not found upon execution of the method.
sgcDatabaseNotFound -20008 Named database file was not found.
sgcLogoNotFound -20009 This error is generated, if the DeleteLogo methods attempts to delete a logo that was not found in the
current (actual) template.
sgcFontNotFound -20010 This error is generated, if the DeleteFont methods

82.2. Protocol Layer
Following error codes could be generated by the protSmartGraphCOM) interface with the tcp/ip network transpocol layer, which connects the SmartCOM (or ort interface. All these error codes indicate abnormal
execution. Most of them indicate problems with the software installation, network or operating system.
Constant Value Description
protOK 1 OK, operation successful.
protUnknownError -15000 Any error condition in protocol engine that is not covered by subsequent error code. This typically indicates software and/or data
transport issues.
protInvalidResponse -15001 Protocol engine received a messis syntactically OK, but which specifies a command that is no valid age block from SmartGraph which
response to the previous command issued by SmartGraphCOM.
protNoResponse -15002 Timeout condition: Protocol engine did not receive any response from SmartGraph within the timeout period.
protNoResponseCommand -15003 Protocol engine received a message block from SmartGraph having no command delimiter character(s).
protResponseTooShort -15004 Protocol engine received a message block from SmartGraph that specifies a total length that cannot fit any useful command.
protInvalidNbArguments -15005 Protocol engine received a message block from SmartGraph having a wrong number or arguments.
protNoCommand -15006 Protocol engine received a message block from SmartGraph having a command with or more white space characters only.
protZeroLenCommand -15007 Protocol engine received a message block from SmartGraph with a command of length Zero
protNonNumericErrorCode -15008 Protocol engine received error message from SmartGraph counterpart, but error code is non-numeric.
protMissingCharacters -15009 Frame of message block indicates more characters nneded than those received.
protTrailingCharacters -15010 Protocol engine detected trailing characters behind correct framed message block
protTimeout -15011 Protocol engine did not receive response from SmartGraph within specified time interval
protFrameError -15012 Protocol engine detected error in protocol framing

82.3. TCP / Transport Layer
Most of the following error conditions indicate severe issues in tcp protocol stack (Microsoft Winsock) or other software malfunction. Most of these errors will intentionally raise a run time error and abort program execution.
The run time error message windows will display the (pof the error condition. Those error coositive) error number and possibly a short description des that are propagated to the protocol engine or the COM interface into
the container application will have negative sign. Following error codes are supported :
Constant Value Description
sckAddressInUse-10048 Port address already in use.
sckAddressNotAvailable -10049 Address not available from the local machine
sckAlreadyComplete -10037 The operation is already completed. No blocking operation in progress
sckAlreadyConnected -10056 Socket is already connected.
sckBadState -10006 Socket state inappropriate for operation.
sckConnectAborted -10053 Connection is aborted due to timeout or other failure
sckConnectionRefused -10061 Connection is forcefully rejected.
sckConnectionReset -10054 The connection is reset by remote side.
sckGetNotSupported -394 The property can't be read.
sckHostNotFound -11001 Authoritative answer: Host not found.
sckHostNotFoundTryAgain -11002 Non-Authoritative answer: Host not found..
sckInProgress -10036 A blocking Winsock operation in progress.
sckInvalidArg -10014 The requested address is a broadcast address, but flag is not set.
sckInvalidOp -10020 Invalid operation at current state.
sckInvalidPropertyValue -380 The property value is invalid.
sckMsgTooBig -10040 The datagram is too large to fit into the buffer and is truncated.
sckNetReset -10052 Connection has timed out when SO_KEEPALIVE is set.
sckNetworkSubsystemFailed -10050 Network subsystem failed.
sckNetworkUnreachable -10051 The network cannot be reached from this host at this time.
sckNoBufferSpace -10055 No buffer space is available.
sckNoData -11004 Valid name, no data record of requested type.
sckNonRecoverableError -11003 Non-recoverable errors.

82.4. TCP Socket States
Upon unsuccessful Connect and Disconnect operations the corresponding method will return the actual tcp socket state, which may be one of the following:
Constant Value Description
sckClosed-12000 Socket is closed.
sckOpen -12001 Socket is open.
sckListening -12002 Socket is listening.
sckConnectionPending -12003 Socket has pending connection.
sckResolvingHost -12004 Socket is resolving host name.
sckHostResolved -12005 Socket has resolved host name.
sckConnecting -12006 Socket is connecting.
sckConnected -12007 Socket is connected.
sckClosing -12008 Socket is closing.
sckError -12009 Socket error occurred

83.2. Abs Command
Description Syntax Settings Return Values Remarks Specifics Example Applies To See Also
Description
The Abs Command template language command sets the absolute position for all subsequent template language commands (graphic primitives) until a new Abs Command is issued.
Syntax
Abs( dblPosX, dblPosY )
The Abs command syntax has these arguments:
Part Description
dblPosXRequired. Constant numeric expression.coordinate) in millimetres. Specifies the horizontal position ( x
dblPosY Required. Constant numeric expression.coordinate) in millimetres. Specifies the horizontal position ( y
Settings
(none)
Return Values
(none)
Remarks
The Rotate
 command also effects previous Abs commands. For instance the sequence of template language commands Rotate(90) Abs(X,Y) Object(ABC) would result in a 90 degrees rotated object ABC at the
position (X,Y). If the sequence of template language commands would be Object(ABC) the result would an object, rotated by 90 degrees Abs(X,Y) Rotate(90) at the position (-Y,X). In order to reset any
effects of previous Rotate commands a sequence rotate(0) abs(0,0) should be issued (see Example
below).
Specifics
(none)

83.5. Feed Command
Description Syntax Settings Return Values Remarks Specifics Example Applies To See Also
Description
The Feed Command template language command sets the spacing factor between individual Lines within all subsequent TextComponent Commands
Syntax
Feed( dblFeedFactor )
The Feed command syntax has these arguments:
Part Description
dblFeedFactorRequired. Numeric expression. Specifies the line spacing factor. Precision: 1 digit right to decimal point. Normal value: 1.5
Settings
(none)
Return Values
(none)
Remarks
In general, line spacing is defined in the font used. It will be automatically increased or decreased with scaling of the font. Default value is 1.5. The Feed Command multiplies this individual (scaling-dependent) spacing
between lines with the factor dblFeedFactor. To reset the line spacintemplate language command. g to normal values, use Feed(1.5)
Line spacing cannot be defined for individual text components (lines) of a TextComponent Command
Spacing will always be applied for the entire Text of a . Line TextComponent Command
.

(none)

Description Syntax Settings Return Values Remarks Specifics Example Applies To See Also
Description
The Font Command template language command sets the font to be used for subsequent Text and TextComponent Commands.
Syntax
Font( strFont, iSystemFont )
The Font command syntax has these arguments:
Part Description
strFontRequired. Constant string expression ennamed font. closed in double quotes. Specifies a
iSystemFontRequired. Integer. 0 or 1 Specifies the use of imported or Windows system fonts.
Settings The settings for the iSystemFont are as given below:
Value Description
0 Uses Windows system font. Name of font is given in strFont parameter.
1 Use imported font. The name of the font within SmartGraph database is given in parameter strFont.
Return Values
(none)
Remarks
The font specified by strFont must already be imported into the actual database if See ImportTTF MethodiSystemFont is set to 1.
 for further information on importing and naming fonts. All templates that are intended to be used in the Laser online database must use imported fonts only. Templates that use Windows system
fonts cant be used in the Laser
Once a font has been specified to used, it will be applied for all subsequent TextComponent Commands. To reset the font to default use Text Commands and Font(STAND-1L) template language

Description Syntax Settings Return Values Remarks Specifics Example Applies To See Also
Description
The Prompt Command template language command defines a text variable to be marked and which can be set by the SetVar
 or SetVarList method.
Syntax
Prompt( iLength, strDefaultText, strName, iPromptOnce )
The Prompt command syntax has these arguments:
Part Description
iLengthRequired. Numeric expression. Specifies the length of the text variable in number of characters.
strDefaultText Required. String expression. Sets the default text to be marked if the variable has not been changed (set) by any SetVar
 or SetVarList method call.
strName Required. String expression. Specifies the references by any name of the variable. This name will be SetVar
 or SetVarList method calls.
iPromptOnce Required. Numeric expression. Specifies marked more than once or not. See if any content of the variable may be Settings
 for further information.
Settings
The iPromptOnce parameter has following settings:
Constant Value Description
sgcPromptOnce 1 Content of the variable can be marked once only. Prior to next marking of the template the variable has to changed to a new value using the
SetVar
 or SetVarList method.
sgcPromptMultiple 0 Content of the variable can be marked more than once.
Return Values

83.18. Scale Command
Description Syntax Settings Return Values Remarks Specifics Example Applies To See Also
Description
The Scale Command template language command scales all subsequent template language commands (graphic primitives) until a new Scale Command is issued.
Syntax
Scale( dblScaleFactorX, dblScaleFactorY )
The Scale command syntax has these arguments:
Part Description
dblScaleFactorXRequired. Constant numeric expression. Specifies the horizontal scaling factor..
dblScaleFactorY Required. Constant numeric expression. Specifies the vertical scaling factor..
Settings
(none)
Return Values
(none)
Remarks
Scaling factors grater 0 but smaller than 1.0 decrease factor above 1.0 incrthe size of subsequent graphic objects, while scaling ease their sizes.
Identical horizontal and vertical scaling factors maintain original aspect ratio.
Negative scaling factors flip (mirror) the objects horizontally or vertically.
A Scale(1,1) Command resets the scaling to default (no scaling).
Specifics
(none)
Example

83.21. TextComponent Command
Description Syntax Settings Return Values Remarks Specifics Example Applies To See Also
Description
The TextComponent template language command creates a single text object with one or more lines of text.
Syntax
BEG_TEXTCOMP strText1 [EOL] [strText2] [EOL] [strText3]  END_TEXTCOMP
The TextComponent command syntax has these arguments:
Part Description
BEG_TEXTCOMP Required. Signals the start of a text component. All text elements to follow will be part of this text object until END_TEXTCOMP is detected.
strText1Required. Text command, Constant string expression enclosed in double quotes. Specifies the first piece of text to be included in the text object.
EOL Optional. Causes the next piece of text to start on the next line (carriage return, line feed).
strText2, strText3, Optional. Text command. Constant string expression enclosed in double quotes. Specifies the second, third,  piece of text to be included in the text object
END_TEXTCOMP Required. Signals the end of a text component. All text elements preceding END_TEXTCOMP will be part of the text object. All text elements to follow will not be
part of this text object.
Settings
(none)
Return Values
(none)
Remarks
Placing EOL delimiters between regular Text commandcommand within a s (see Text Command) instead of between text TextComponent will have no effect. EOL will be ignored than. Each Text Command will
be placed at the actual absolute position.

84.5. HTML JavaScript
The JavaScript integration is one the most simple development environment or other large ActiveX enabl(client side) integration that does not require any ed packages such as Office. Just an ActiveX enabled
browser like Microsoft Internet Explorer. Make sure the browser is enabled for AxtiveX and the security settings of the browser allow for AxtiveX
execution.
In order to write a first Hello World use any text editor and enter following lines of code:
<html> <head>
<meta http-equiv="Content-Type" content="text/html; charset=windows-1252"> <title>Hello World</title>
</head> <body>
<script language=javascript> var sg;
 sg = new ActiveXObject("SmartGraphCOM.CSmartGraph"); sg.Connect("","","");
 sg.ConnectOfflineDB("c:\\Standard.ydb"); sg.CreateTemplate("MyTemplate","Paper","Allprint",true);
 sg.AddTemplateText("MyTemplate", " Abs(-30,0) Height(8) \"Hello World\" "); sg.Disconnect();
</script> </body>
</html>
and save the files as HelloWorld.htm
The gray colored code is needed for providing a syntaprocessed by the browser. This is standard for all ctical correct HTML page in order to be properly web pages. Only the few red lines of code are needed to
establish a first Hello World application.
Please note that this examples assumes that a SmartGrroot directory of the C-drive. This path can be chanaph database named Standard.ydb is located in the ged to any other location that specifies a database. Upon

84.6. PERL
The PERL integration is a very simple method of integration. As a precondition the PERL interpreter environment must be set up in Windows environment.
All that is needed is a text editor to write following lines of code into a new file named HelloWorld.pl
use strict; use Win32::OLE;
my $sg = Win32::OLE->new("SmartGraphCOM.CSmartGraph"); my $lRet;
$lRet = $sg->Connect(); $lRet = $sg->CreateTemplate("MyTemplate", "Paper", "Allprint", 1);
$lRet = $sg->SetTemplateText("MyTemplate", "Abs(-30,0) Height(8) \"Hello World!\" "); $lRet = $sg->Disconnect();
The gray colored code is needed to enable ActiveX support to the PERL interpreter.
Please note that this examples assumes that a SmartGrroot directory of the C-drive. This path can be chanaph database named Standard.ydb is located in the ged to any other location that specifies a database. Upon
SmartGraph installation (setup) a basic database directory. This databse could be copied of the named Standard.ydb is placed into the installation path could be changed to point to that location.
Start SmartGraph (which always must be running in order to use SmartGraphCOM functions) and run the PERL script file by double-clicking (in explorer or other file shell tool). The SmartGraph template editor
window shows the result as follows:

84.7. TCL
The TCL integration is a very simple method of integration. As a precondition the TCL interpreter v8.1.x or higher environment is required and must be set up in Windows
environment.
All that is needed is a text editor to write following lines of code into a new file named HelloWorld.tcl
package require tcom
set sg [::tcom::ref createobject "SmartGraphCOM.CSmartGraph"] set lRet [$sg Connect]
set lRet [$sg ConnectOfflineDB "C:\\standard.ydb"] set lRet [$sg CreateTemplate "MyTemplate" "Paper" "Allprint" 1]
set lRet [$sg SetTemplateText "MyTemplate" "Abs(-30,0) Height(8) \"Hello World!\" "] set lRet [$sg Disconnect]
The gray colored code is needed to enable ActiveX support to the TCL interpreter.
Please note that this examples assumes that a SmartGrroot directory of the C-drive. This path can be chanaph database named Standard.ydb is located in the ged to any other location that specifies a database. Upon
SmartGraph installation (setup) a basic database directory. This databse could be copied of the named Standard.ydb is placed into the installation path could be changed to point to that location.
Start SmartGraph (which always must be running in order to use SmartGraphCOM functions) and run the TCL script file by double-clicking (in explorer or other file shell tool). The SmartGraph template editor window
shows the result as follows:

84.10. JScript
The JScript integration is one the most simple integration that does not require or other large ActiveX enabled packages such as Officeany development environment . Just enable Windows scripting (if not enabled already
by default)
In order to write a first Hello World use any text editor and enter following lines of code
var sg; sg = new ActiveXObject("SmartGraphCOM.CSmartGraph");
sg.Connect("","",""); sg.ConnectOfflineDB("c:\\standard.ydb");
sg.CreateTemplate("HelloWorld","Paper","Allprint",true); sg.AddTemplateText("HelloWorld", "Abs(-30,0) Height(8) \"Hello World\" ");
sg.Disconnect();
and save the file as HelloWorld.js
Please note that this examples assumes that a SmartGrroot directory of the C-drive. This path can be chanaph database named Standard.ydb is located in the ged to any other location that specifies a database. Upon
SmartGraph installation (setup) a basic database directory. This databse could be copied of the named Standard.ydb is placed into the installation path could be changed to point to that location.
Start SmartGraph (which always must be running in script by double clicking in MS fileorder to use SmartGraphCOM functions) and start the explorer). The SmartGraph template editor window shows the result as
follows

84.11. VBScript
The VBScript integration is one the most simple inenvironment or other large ActiveX enabled packages sutegration that does not require any development ch as Office. Just enable Windows scripting (if not
enabled already by default)
In order to write a first Hello World use any text editor and enter following lines of code
set sg = CreateObject("SmartGraphCOM.CSmartGraph") lRet = sg.Connect("","","")
lRet = sg.ConnectOfflineDB("c:\\standard.ydb") lRet = sg.CreateTemplate("HelloWorld","Paper","Allprint",true)
lRet = sg.AddTemplateText("HelloWorld", "Abs(-30,0) Height(8) ""Hello World"" ") lRet = sg.Disconnect();
and save the file as HelloWorld.vbs
Please note that this examples assumes that a SmartGrroot directory of the C-drive. This path can be chanaph database named Standard.ydb is located in the ged to any other location that specifies a database. Upon
SmartGraph installation (setup) a basic database directory. This databse could be copied of the named Standard.ydb is placed into the installation path could be changed to point to that location.
